>>> STEP 1: UNPLUG the USB cable.
    OK - device off the USB bus.

>>> STEP 2: SHUT DOWN ANDROID PROPERLY if it is running (hold Power ->
           Power off). A clean unmount keeps the ext4 journal clean, which
           matters when we edit files in place later.
>>> STEP 3: Pull the BATTERY, wait ~10s, reinsert it (the power button does
           not cold-boot this device, and BootROM only appears on a cold boot).
>>> STEP 4: Hold VOLUME UP and plug the USB cable back in.
